successors = {node: [] for node in nodes}\n    indegree = {node: 0 for node in nodes}\n    for parent, child in edges:\n        if parent not in successors or child not in indegree:\n            return False\n        successors[parent].append(child)\n        indegree[child] += 1\n    queue = [node for node, degree in indegree.items() if degree == 0]\n    visited = 0\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop()\n        visited += 1\n        for child in successors[node]:\n            indegree[child] -= 1\n            if indegree[child] == 0:\n                queue.append(child)\n calculated_flips[\"q2\"]) > probability_tolerance:\n            raise ValueError(\"Q2 决策翻转率与逐次记录不一致\")\n        policies = _q4_policy_record(result, \"q3\")\n        reported_q3 = _first(policies[\"point\"], (\"flip_rate\", \"point_flip_rate\"))\n        if reported_q3 is None or abs(float(reported_q3) - calculated_flips[\"q3\"]) > probability_tolerance:\n            raise ValueError(\"Q3 条件网络决策翻转率与逐次记录不一致\")\n        checks.append({\"id\": \"RC-20--RC-30\", \"ok\": True})\n    except (IndexError, KeyError, RuntimeError, StopIteration, TypeError, ValueError, ZeroDivisionError) as exc:\n        errors.append(f\"Q4: {exc}\")\n\n\ndef validate_constraints(results: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"从落盘结果对象重新计算约束；不读取或信任求解器的 \"problem2\": _named_mapping(results, (\"problem2\",)) or {},\n        \"problem3\": _named_mapping(results, (\"problem3\",)) or {},\n        \"problem4\": _named_mapping(results, (\"problem4\",)) or {},\n    }\n    validators = (\n        (\"Q1\", _validate_q1, problem_results[\"problem1\"]),\n        (\"Q2\", _validate_q2, problem_results[\"problem2\"]),\n        (\"Q3\", _validate_q3, problem_results[\"problem3\"]),\n        (\"Q4\", _validate_q4, problem_results[\"problem4\"]),\n    )\n    for name, validator, payload in validators:\n        before = len(errors)\n        try:\n            validator(payload, errors, checks)\n        except Exception as exc:  # 独立约束复算必须阻断未归类异常\n            errors.append(f\"{name}: 约束复算器异常 {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n        if len(errors) == before and not any(check[\"id\"].startswith(name) for check in checks):\n            checks.append({\"id\": name, \"ok\": True})\n    return {\n        \"ok\": not errors,\n        \"checks\": A leaf has only an inspection decision.  The\n# finished node has all four combinations because an undetected return can\n# still be dismantled.\nQ3_LEAF_OPTIONS = ((0,), (1,))\nQ3_SEMI_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS = ((0, 0), (1, 0), (1, 1))\nQ3_ROOT_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS = ((0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0), (1, 1))\nQ3_POLICY_DECISION_COUNT = len(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS) * 2\nQ3_POLICY_UPPER_BOUND = 2 ** Q3_POLICY_DECISION_COUNT\nQ3_H1_EFFECTIVE_POLICY_COUNT = (\n    (2 ** len(PART_IDS))\n    * (len(Q3_SEMI_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS) ** len(SEMI_IDS))\n    * len(Q3_ROOT_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS)\n)\nQ3_POLICY_COUNT = Q3_H1_EFFECTIVE_POLICY_COUNT\nQ3_H1_TOPOLOGY_STATUS = Q3_TOPOLOGY_CLAIM_LABEL\n\n\ndef iter_h1_effective_policies() -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Enumerate the effective H1 policy space, not the unpruned upper bound.\"\"\"\n    for leaf_values in product(Q3_LEAF_OPTIONS, repeat=len(PART_IDS)):\n        leaf_decisions = {\n            part_id: int(values[0])\n            for part_id, {\"inspect\": int(values[0]), \"disassemble\": int(values[1])}\n                for semi_id, values in zip(SEMI_IDS, semi_values)\n            }\n            for root_values in Q3_ROOT_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS:\n                yield {\n                    \"leaves\": leaf_decisions,\n                    \"semiproducts\": semi_decisions,\n                    FINISHED_PRODUCT_ID: {\n                        \"inspect\": int(root_values[0]),\n                        \"disassemble\": int(root_values[1]),\n                    },\n                }\n\n\ndef partitions_into_three_groups(\n    elements: Sequence[Any],\n) -> Iterator[Tuple[Tuple[Any, ...], Tuple[Any, ...], Tuple[Any, ...]]]:\n    \"\"\"Yield each unlabeled three-part partition once, preserving group labels.\"\"\"\n    ordered = tuple(elements)\n    if len(ordered) < Q3_SEMI_PRODUCT_COUNT_CONDITIONAL:\n        return\n    first, rest = ordered[0], ordered[1:]\n    for middle_size in range(1, len(rest)):\n        for middle in product(rest, repeat=middle_size):\n            if len(set(middle)) != middle_size or rest[0] not in middle:\n                continue\n            middle_set = set(middle)\n            third = tuple(item for item in rest if item not in middle_set)\n            if third:\n                yield (first,), tuple(middle), third\n\n\ndef validate_explicit_topology(\n    children_by_parent: Mapping[Any, Sequence[Any]],\n    part_ids: Sequence[Any],\n    semiproduct_ids: Sequence[Any],\n    finished_id: Any,\n) -> Dict[Any, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Validate an explicitly supplied arbitrary m,n assembly DAG.\"\"\"\n    all_nonroot = tuple(part_ids) + tuple(semiproduct_ids)\n    all_nodes = all_nonroot + (finished_id,)\n    if len(set(all_nodes)) != len(all_nodes):\n        raise ValueError(\"assembly node ids must be unique\")\n    if finished_id not in children_by_parent:\n        raise ValueError(\"explicit topology must include the finished-product parent\")\n    parent_by_child: Dict[Any, Any] = {}\n    children_seen = set()\n    for parent, children in children_by_parent.items():\n        if parent not in all_nodes:\n            raise ValueError(f\"unknown parent node: {parent!r}\")\n        for child in children:\n            if child not in all_nodes:\n                raise ValueError(f\"unknown child node: {child!r}\")\n            if child == parent:\n                raise ValueError(f\"self-loop at {parent!r}\")\n            if child in parent_by_child:\n                raise ValueError(f\"child has more than one parent: {child!r}\")\n            parent_by_child[child] = parent\n            children_seen.add(child)\n    if set(parent_by_child) != set(all_nonroot):\n        missing = sorted(set(all_nonroot) - set(parent_by_child), key=str)\n        raise ValueError(f\"nonroot nodes without exactly one parent: {missing}\")\n    if set(children_by_parent[finished_id]) != set(semiproduct_ids):\n        raise ValueError(\"finished product must consume exactly the declared semiproducts\")\n\n    indegree = {node: 0 for node in all_nodes}\n    for parent, children in children_by_parent.items():\n        for child in children:\n            indegree[parent] += 1\n    queue = [node for node, degree in indegree.items() if degree == 0]\n    visited = []\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop()\n        visited.append(node)\n        for child in children_by_parent.get(node, ()):\n            indegree[child] -= 1\n            if indegree[child] == 0:\n                queue.append(child)\n    if len(visited) != len(all_nodes):\n        raise ValueError(\"explicit assembly topology contains a directed cycle\")\n    return {\n        \"nodes\": tuple(all_nodes),\n        \"parent_by_child\": dict(parent_by_child),\n        \"children_by_parent\": {key: tuple(value) for key, value in children_by_parent.items()},\n        \"is_dag\": True,\n    }\n\n\ndef validate_h1_topology() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    return validate_explicit_topology(\n        H1_CHILDREN_BY_ID, PART_IDS, SEMI_IDS, FINISHED_PRODUCT_ID\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Question 4: exact interval and sampling utilities\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nQ4_SAMPLING_CENTER_RULE = \"initial_synthetic_xi_over_n\"\nQ4_SCENARIO_LABEL = \"synthetic_scenario_no_observed_samples\"\nQ4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2 = Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE / Q2_RATE_NODE_COUNT\nQ4_ALPHA_NODE_Q3 = Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE / Q3_TOTAL_NODE_COUNT_CONDITIONAL\nQ4_JOINT_COVERAGE_LOWER_BOUND = (\n    ONE - Q2_RATE_NODE_COUNT * Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2\n)\n\n\ndef clopper_pearson_interval(\n    n: int, x: int, alpha: float\n) -> Tuple[float, float]:\n    \"\"\"Return the exact CP interval with explicit zero/all-defect branches.\"\"\"\n    from scipy.stats import beta\n\n    if int(n) != n or int(x) != x:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP sample size and defect count must be integers\")\n    n = int(n)\n    x = int(x)\n    if n <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP sample size must be positive\")\n    if x < 0 or x > n:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP defect count must lie in [0,n]\")\n    alpha = validate_probability(alpha, \"CP alpha\")\n    if not 0.0 < alpha < ONE:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP alpha must lie strictly between zero and one\")\n    if x == 0:\n        return 0.0, float((ONE - alpha) ** (ONE / n))\n    if x == n:\n        return float(alpha ** (ONE / n)), ONE\n    lower = float(beta.ppf(alpha / 2.0, x, n - x + 1))\n    upper = float(beta.ppf(ONE - alpha / 2.0, x + 1, n - x))\n    return lower, upper\n\n\ndef expected_cp_halfwidth(n: int, p: float, alpha: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute E_CP(n,p,alpha), the expected CP interval half-width.\"\"\"\n    if int(n) != n or int(n) <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP sample size must be a positive integer\")\n    n = int(n)\n    p = validate_probability(p, \"CP scenario rate\")\n    alpha = validate_probability(alpha, \"CP alpha\")\n    if not 0.0 < alpha < ONE:\n        raise ValueError(\"CP alpha must lie strictly between zero and one\")\n    expected_width = 0.0\n    for x in range(n + 1):\n        probability = math.comb(n, x) * (p ** x) * ((ONE - p) ** (n - x))\n        lower, upper = clopper_pearson_interval(n, x, alpha)\n        expected_width += probability * (upper - lower)\n    return expected_width / 2.0\n\n\ndef minimum_cp_sample_size(\n    p: float,\n    alpha_node: float,\n    target_halfwidth: float = Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH,\n    search_cap: int = INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP,\n) -> Optional[int]:\n    \"\"\"Return the first integer n satisfying the node-specific CP criterion.\"\"\"\n    if int(search_cap) != search_cap or int(search_cap) <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"integer search cap must be a positive integer\")\n    target = validate_probability(target_halfwidth, \"target CP half-width\")\n    for n in range(1, int(search_cap) + 1):\n        if expected_cp_halfwidth(n, p, alpha_node) <= target + PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n            return n\n    return None\n\n\ndef make_rng(seed: int = RANDOM_SEED):\n    \"\"\"Create the fixed NumPy generator required by the sampling contract.\"\"\"\n    import numpy as np\n\n    return np.random.default_rng(int(seed))\n\n\ndef draw_binomial_counts(\n    rates: Mapping[str, float],\n    sample_sizes: Mapping[str, int],\n    rng: Any,\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    if set(rates) != set(sample_sizes):\n        raise ValueError(\"rate and sample-size node ids must match\")\n    counts = {}\n    for node_id in rates:\n        p = validate_probability(rates[node_id], f\"{node_id}.sampling_rate\")\n        n = int(sample_sizes[node_id])\n        if n <= 0:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{node_id} sample size must be positive\")\n        counts[node_id] = int(rng.binomial(n, p))\n    return counts\n\n\ndef point_estimates(\n    counts: Mapping[str, int], sample_sizes: Mapping[str, int]\n) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    if set(counts) != set(sample_sizes):\n        raise ValueError(\"count and sample-size node ids must match\")\n    estimates = {}\n    for node_id, count in counts.items():\n        n = int(sample_sizes[node_id])\n        if n <= 0 or int(count) != count or int(count) < 0 or int(count) > n:\n            raise ValueError(f\"invalid count/sample pair for {node_id}\")\n        estimates[node_id] = int(count) / n\n    return estimates\n\n\ndef sampling_audit_records(\n    counts:       \"status\": \"open\",\n                \"action\": Q4_SCENARIO_LABEL,\n            },\n        ),\n        \"exit_code\": 1 if errors else 0,\n    }\n\n\ndef run_all(path: Any = FACTS_FILENAME) annotations\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport params\n\n\nREQUIRED_TOP_FIELDS = (\"stage\", \"source_file\", \"purpose\", \"facts\")\nREQUIRED_FACT_FIELDS = field is not None:\n        record[\"field\"] = field\n    return record\n\n\ndef _is_number(value):\n    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return False\n    try:\n        return math.isfinite(float(value))\n    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef _rate(value, label, fact_id, errors, field):\n    if isinstance(value, bool):\n        errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_rate\", f\"{label} 不是数值\", fact_id, field))\n        return None\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        text = value.strip()\n        if not text.endswith(\"%\"):\n            errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_rate\", f\"{label} 不是百分数字符串\", fact_id, field))\n            return None\n        try:\n            rate = float(text[:-1].strip()) / params.PERCENT_DENOMINATOR\n        except (TypeError, ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):\n            errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_rate\", f\"{label} 无法解析\", fact_id, field))\n            return None\n    elif _is_number(value):\n        rate = float(value)\n    else:\n        errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_rate\", f\"{label} 类型错误\", fact_id, field))\n        return None\n    if not math.isfinite(rate) or rate < 0.0 or rate > 1.0:\n        errors.append(\n            _issue(\"rate_out_of_domain\", f\"{label} 不在 [0,1]\", fact_id, field)\n        )\n        return None\n    return rate\n\n\ndef _money(value, label, fact_id, errors, field):\n    if not _is_number(value):\n        errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_money\", f\"{label} 不是有限数值\", fact_id, field))\n        return None\n    amount = float(value)\n    if amount < 0.0:\n        errors.append(_issue(\"negative_money\", f\"{label} 为负\", fact_id, field))\n        return None\n    return amount\n\n\ndef _mapping(value):\n    return isinstance(value, dict)\n\n\ndef _require_fields(container, fields, label, fact_id, errors):\n    if not _mapping(container):\n        errors.append(_issue(\"not_object\", f\"{label} 不是对象\", fact_id))\n        return False\n    missing = [field for field in fields if field not in container]\n    if missing:\n        errors.append(\n            _issue(\n                \"missing_fields\",\n                f\"{label} 缺少字段: {', '.join(missing)}\",\n                fact_id,\n            )\n        )\n        return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef validate_shape(payload):\n    errors = []\n    if not _mapping(payload):\n        errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_top_level\", \"顶层 JSON 必须是对象\"))\n        return errors, [], {}\n\n    missing_top = [field for field in REQUIRED_TOP_FIELDS if field not in payload]\n    if missing_top:\n        errors.append(\n            _issue(\"missing_top_fields\", f\"顶层缺少字段: {', '.join(missing_top)}\")\n        )\n\n    records = payload.get(\"facts\")\n    if not isinstance(records, list) or not records:\n        errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_facts\", \"facts 必须是非空数组\"))\n        return errors, [], {}\n\n    indexed = {}\n    for position, record in enumerate(records):\n        if not _mapping(record):\n            errors.append(_issue(\"invalid_fact_record\", f\"facts[{position}] 不是对象\"))\n            continue\n        fact_id = record.get(\"id\")\n        if not isinstance(fact_id, str) or not fact_id.strip():\n            errors.append(\n                _issue(\"missing_fact_id\", f\"facts[{position}] 缺少规范字段 id\")\n            )\n            continue\n        missing_fields = [\n            field for field in REQUIRED_FACT_FIELDS if field not in 概率网格越界\")\n    return sorted(set(rates))\n\n\ndef _fixed_threshold(n, direction, p0, alpha, beta, delta):\n    \"\"\"在一个样本量上枚举全部合法整数阈值。\"\"\"\n    if direction == \"reject\":\n        alternative = p0 + delta\n        if not 0.0 <= alternative <= 1.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"拒收备择率不在 [0,1]\")\n        cutoff = np.arange(n, dtype=int)\n        nominal_cdf = scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, p0)\n        alternative_cdf = scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, alternative)\n        feasible = (\n            (nominal_cdf >= 1.0 - alpha - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n            & (alternative_cdf >= 1.0 - beta - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n        )\n        positions = np.flatnonzero(feasible)\n        if positions.size == 0:\n            return None\n        return int(cutoff[positions[0]] + 1)\n\n    if direction == \"accept\":\n        alternative = p0 - delta\n        if not 0.0 <= alternative <= 1.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"接收备择率不在 [0,1]\")\n        cutoff = np.arange(n + 1, dtype=int)\n        nominal_cdf = scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, p0)\n        alternative_cdf = scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, alternative)\n        feasible = (\n            (nominal_cdf >= 1.0 - alpha - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n            & (alternative_cdf >= 1.0 - beta - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n        )\n        positions = np.flatnonzero(feasible)\n        if positions.size == 0:\n            return None\n        return int(cutoff[positions[-1]])\n\n    raise ValueError(f\"未知判定方向: {direction}\")\n\n\ndef exact_integer_enumeration(\n    direction,\n    p0,\n    alpha,\n    beta,\n    delta,\n    search_cap=INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP,\n):\n    \"\"\"按样本量递增作精确整数枚举，并执行上游规定的破平规则。\"\"\"\n    if search_cap < 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"整数搜索上限必须为正\")\n    smaller_n_checked = 0\n    for n in range(1, search_cap + 1):\n        threshold = _fixed_threshold(n, direction, p0, alpha, beta, delta)\n        if threshold is None:\n            smaller_n_checked += 1\n            continue\n        alternative = p0 + delta if direction == \"reject\" else p0 - delta\n        cutoff = threshold - 1 if direction == \"reject\" else threshold\n        decision_probability = float(\n            scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, p0)\n        )\n        if direction == \"reject\":\n            decision_probability = 1.0 - decision_probability\n        power = float(scipy.stats.binom.cdf(cutoff, n, alternative))\n        if direction == \"reject\":\n            power = 1.0 - power\n        return {\n            \"direction\": direction,\n            \"n\": int(n),\n            \"c\":       \"all_smaller_n_infeasible\": True,\n            \"search_cap\": int(search_cap),\n            \"feasible\": True,\n        }\n    raise RuntimeError(\"整数搜索上限内不存在可行阈值\")\n\n\ndef _cached_design(direction, alpha, beta, delta):\n    key = (direction, float(alpha), float(beta), float(delta))\n    if key not in _SENSITIVITY_CACHE:\n        _SENSITIVITY_CACHE[key] = exact_integer_enumeration(\n            direction, P0, alpha, beta, delta\n        )\n    return dict(_SENSITIVITY_CACHE[key])\n\n\ndef _manual_binomial_cdf(n, cutoff, probability):\n    \"\"\"独立于 scipy 的逐项求和，用于最终账本复算。\"\"\"\n    if cutoff < 0:\n        return 0.0\n    if cutoff >= n:\n        return 1.0\n    if probability == 0.0:\n        return 1.0\n    if probability == 1.0:\n        return 0.0\n    total = 0.0\n    for count in range(int(cutoff) + 1):\n        log_probability = (\n            math.lgamma(n + 1)\n            - math.lgamma(count + 1)\n            - math.lgamma(n - count + 1)\n            + count * math.log(probability)\n            + (n - count) * math.log1p(-probability)\n        )\n        total += math.exp(log_probability)\n    return min(1.0, max(0.0, total))\n\n\ndef _stop_metrics(max_samples, accept_boundary, reject_boundary, probability, terminal_action):\n    \"\"\"有界整数边界 DP；期望量按每轮新增终止概率显式累加。\"\"\"\n    if max_samples < 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"SPRT 上限必须为正\")\n    if reject_boundary <= accept_boundary:\n        raise ValueError(\"SPRT 拒收界必须高于接收界\")\n    if terminal_action not in (0, 1):\n        raise ValueError(\"terminal_action 必须为 0 或 1\")\n    if not 0.0 <= probability <= 1.0:\n        raise ValueError(\"SPRT 单步概率越界\")\n\n    state = [1.0]\n    expected_samples = 0.0\n    accept_probability = 0.0\n    reject_probability = 0.0\n    forced_probability = 0.0\n    stop_times = []\n    sample_indices = []\n    accept_masses = []\n    reject_masses = []\n    unresolved_masses = []\n    expected_increments = []\n\n    for sample_index in range(1, max_samples + 1):\n        entering_probability = sum(state)\n        increment = sample_index * entering_probability\n        expected_samples += increment\n        next_state = [0.0] * (sample_index + 1)\n        for defect_count, mass in enumerate(state):\n            next_state[defect_count] += mass * (1.0 - probability)\n            next_state[defect_count + 1] += mass * probability\n\n        accept_end = accept_boundary + 1 if accept_boundary >= 0 else 0\n        accepted_now = sum(next_state[:accept_end])\n        rejected_now = sum(next_state[reject_boundary:])\n        pending_start = max(0, accept_end)\n        pending_end = max(pending_start, reject_boundary)\n        pending = [0.0] * len(next_state)\n        for count in range(pending_start, min(pending_end, len(next_state))):\n            pending[count] = next_state[count]\n\n        unresolved_now = sum(pending)\n        if sample_index == max_samples:\n            forced_probability += unresolved_now\n            if terminal_action == 0:\n                accepted_now += unresolved_now\n            else:\n                rejected_now += unresolved_now\n            pending = [0.0] * len(next_state)\n\n        accept_probability += accepted_now\n        reject_probability += rejected_now\n        sample_indices.append(sample_index)\n        accept_masses.append(accepted_now)\n        reject_masses.append(rejected_now)\n        unresolved_masses.append(unresolved_now)\n        expected_increments.append(increment)\n        state = pending\n\n    stop_times = {\n        \"sample_index\": sample_indices,\n        \"accept_probability\": accept_masses,\n        \"reject_probability\": reject_masses,\n        \"unresolved_before_terminal_action\": unresolved_masses,\n        \"expected_increment\": expected_increments,\n    }\n    return {\n        \"accept_probability\": float(accept_probability),\n        \"reject_probability\": float(reject_probability),\n        \"expected_samples\": float(expected_samples),\n        \"truncation_rate\": float(forced_probability),\n        \"forced_terminal_probability\": float(forced_probability),\n        \"stop_distribution\": stop_times,\n    }\n\n\ndef _direct_stop_metrics(\n    max_samples, accept_boundary, reject_boundary, probability, terminal_action\n):\n    \"\"\"按首次停止时刻直接递推，独立复核向量状态 DP。\"\"\"\n    records = {\n        sample: {\n            \"accept_probability\": 0.0,\n            \"reject_probability\": 0.0,\n            \"forced_probability\": 0.0,\n        }\n        for sample in range(1, max_samples + 1)\n    }\n\n    def walk(sample_index, defect_count, probability_mass):\n        if sample_index > 0 and defect_count <= accept_boundary:\n            records[sample_index][\"accept_probability\"] += probability_mass\n            return\n        if sample_index > 0 and defect_count >= reject_boundary:\n            records[sample_index][\"reject_probability\"] += probability_mass\n            return\n        if sample_index == max_samples:\n            key = \"accept_probability\" if terminal_action == 0 else \"reject_probability\"\n            records[sample_index][key] += probability_mass\n            records[sample_index][\"forced_probability\"] += probability_mass\n            return\n        walk(\n            sample_index + 1,\n            defect_count,\n            probability_mass * (1.0 - probability),\n        )\n        walk(\n            sample_index + 1,\n            defect_count + 1,\n            probability_mass * probability,\n        )\n\n    walk(0, 0, 1.0)\n    expected_samples = 0.0\n    accept_probability = 0.0\n    reject_probability = 0.0\n    forced_probability = 0.0\n    sample_indices = []\n    accept_masses = []\n    reject_masses = []\n    forced_masses = []\n    for sample_index in range(1, max_samples + 1):\n        row = records[sample_index]\n        expected_samples += sample_index * (\n            row[\"accept_probability\"]\n            + row[\"reject_probability\"]\n            + row[\"forced_probability\"]\n        )\n        accept_probability += row[\"accept_probability\"]\n        reject_probability += row[\"reject_probability\"]\n        forced_probability += row[\"forced_probability\"]\n        sample_indices.append(sample_index)\n        accept_masses.append(row[\"accept_probability\"])\n \"reject_probability\": float(reject_probability),\n        \"expected_samples\": float(expected_samples),\n        \"truncation_rate\": float(forced_probability),\n      \"reject_probability\": reject_masses,\n            \"forced_terminal_probability\": forced_masses,\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef _ceil_quantile(probability, n, rate):\n    value = float(scipy.stats.binom.ppf(probability, n, rate))\n    if not math.isfinite(value):\n        return 0\n    return max(0, min(n, int(math.ceil(value))))\n\n\ndef _candidate_boundaries(max_samples, direction, fixed_design, alpha, beta, delta):\n    if direction == \"reject\":\n        alternative = P0 + delta\n        accept_center = max(\n            _ceil_quantile(1.0 - alpha, max_samples, min(max_samples + 1, reject_center + offset))\n        for offset in SPRT_BOUNDARY_OFFSETS\n    }\n    candidates = {\n        (accept_boundary, reject_boundary, terminal_action)\n        for metrics[\"accept_probability\"]\n            >= 1.0 - alpha - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n        )\n        power = metrics[\"power\"]\n        second_pass = power >= 1.0 - beta - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n    return first_pass and second_pass\n\n\ndef bounded_sprt_dp(direction, alpha, beta, delta, fixed_design):\n    \"\"\"以精确分位数种子枚举整数边界，再用首次停止时刻 DP 校准。\"\"\"\n    alternative = P0 + delta if direction == \"disassemble_and_repeat\" if disassemble == 1 else \"scrap_and_restart\",\n    )\n    if item is not None:\n        outgoing.append(item)\n    return outgoing\n\n\ndef _compile_graph(pars, strategy):\n    initial = (\"fresh\", 0, (ABSENT, ABSENT))\n    states = [initial]\n    state_index = {initial: 0}\n    transitions = []\n    terminal_probability = []\n    cursor = 0\n    while cursor < len(states):\n        state = states[cursor]\n        outgoing = _outgoing(state, pars, strategy)\n        transitions.append(outgoing)\n        terminal_probability.append(sum(item[\"probability\"] for item in outgoing if item[\"next_state\"] is None))\n        for item in outgoing:\n            target = item[\"next_state\"]\n            if terminal_probability, state_index[initial]\n\n\ndef _unproductive_states(transitions, terminal_probability):\n    success_reachable = {index for index, probability in enumerate(terminal_probability) if probability > 0.0}\n    changed = True\n    while changed:\n        changed = False\n        for source, outgoing in enumerate(transitions):\n            if source in success_reachable:\n                continue\n            if any(item[\"next_state\"] is not None and item[\"next_state\"] in success_reachable for item in outgoing):\n                success_reachable.add(source)\n                changed = True\n    return sorted(set(range(len(transitions))) - success_reachable)\n\n\ndef _state_label(state):\n    phase, index, statuses = state\n    status_text = \",\".join(\"absent\" if value == ABSENT else (\"good\" if value == GOOD else \"defective\") for value in statuses)\n    return \"{}:{}:[{}]\".format(phase, index, status_text)\n\n\ndef renewal_bellman_solver(transitions, terminal_probability, initial_index):\n    size = len(transitions)\n    lhs = np.eye(size, dtype=float)\n    rhs = np.zeros((size, len(COST_NAMES) + int(ONE)), dtype=float)\n    rhs[:, 0] = np.asarray(terminal_probability, dtype=float)\n    for source, outgoing in enumerate(transitions):\n        for item in outgoing:\n            probability = item[\"probability\"]\n            if -probability_tolerance) or np.any(success > ONE + probability_tolerance):\n        raise RuntimeError(\"Bellman success probability outside its domain\")\n    if np.any(success <= 0.0):\n        raise initial_index\n\n\ndef event_cashflow_ledger(transitions, states, initial_index):\n    records = []\n    for source, outgoing in enumerate(transitions):\n        expected_action_cost = sum(item[\"probability\"] * sum(item[\"costs\"].values()) for item in outgoing)\n        for item in outgoing:\n            cash = float(sum(item[\"costs\"].values()))\n            records.append({\n                \"event_type\": item[\"event_type\"],\n                \"physical_node\": item[\"physical_node\"],\n                \"inspection_evidence\": item[\"inspection_evidence\"],\n                \"strategy_branch\": item[\"strategy_branch\"],\n                \"probability\": item[\"probability\"],\n                \"cash_term\": -cash,\n                \"cumulative_cost\": tail_threshold = PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL * PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n    while pending and max(pending.values()) > tail_threshold and rounds < int(INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP if \"INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP\" in globals() else params.INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP):\n        next_pending = {}\n        for source, mass in pending.items():\n            for item in transitions[source]:\n                event_probability = mass * item[\"probability\"]\n                for name in COST_NAMES:\n                    expected_costs[name] += event_probability * item[\"costs\"][name]\n                if item[\"next_state\"] is None:\n                    success_probability += event_probability\n                else:\n                    next_pending[item[\"next_state\"]] = next_pending.get(item[\"next_state\"], 0.0) + event_probability\n        pending = next_pending\n        rounds += int(ONE)\n    unresolved_probability = float(sum(pending.values()))\n    return {\n        \"records\": records,\n        \"expected_costs\": expected_costs,\n        \"success_probability\": float(success_probability),\n        \"propagation_rounds\": rounds,\n        \"unresolved_probability\": unresolved_probability,\n    }\n\n\ndef _infeasible_result(strategy, reason, state_count, transition_count):\n    return {\n        \"feasible\": False,\n        \"reason\": reason,\n        \"strategy_tuple\": tuple(int(value) for value in strategy),\n        \"strategy\": list(strategy),\n        \"strategy_dict\": _strategy_dict(strategy),\n        \"success_probability\": None,\n \"event_ledger\": [],\n        \"ledger_summary\": {\"unresolved_probability\": ONE},\n        \"salvage_credit_once\": bool(SALVAGE_CREDIT_ONCE),\n    }\n\n\ndef evaluate_strategy(raw_parameters, strategy):\n    strategy = tuple(int(value) for value in strategy)\n    if len(strategy) != len(Q2_DECISION_NAMES) or any(value not in BINARY_DECISIONS for value in strategy):\n        raise ValueError(\"invalid Q2 strategy\")\n    pars = _case_parameters(raw_parameters)\n    states, transitions, terminal_probability, initial_index = _compile_graph(pars, strategy)\n    transition_count = expected_costs = {name: float(costs[initial_index, index]) for index, name in enumerate(COST_NAMES)}\n    ledger_costs = ledger[\"expected_costs\"]\n    component_gaps = [abs(expected_costs[name] - ledger_costs[name]) for name in COST_NAMES]\n    ledger_profit = (pars[\"sale_price\"] * ledger[\"success_probability\"] - sum(ledger_costs.values())) / ledger[\"success_probability\"]\n    pi_try = pars[\"sale_price\"] * h_initial - sum(expected_costs.values())\n    profit_per_good = pi_try / h_initial\n    profit_gap = abs(profit_per_good - ledger_profit)\n    success_gap = abs(h_initial - \"feasible\": True,\n        \"reason\": \"finite_renewal_solution\",\n        \"strategy_tuple\": strategy,\n        \"strategy\": list(strategy),\n        \"strategy_dict\": _strategy_dict(strategy),\n        \"success_probability\": h_initial,\n        \"pi_try\": pi_try,\n        \"profit_per_good\": profit_per_good,\n        \"profit\": profit_per_good,\n        \"expected_profit\": profit_per_good,\n        \"expected_cost_breakdown\": expected_costs,\n        \"cost_breakdown\": unit_breakdown,\n        \"cashflow_gap\": cashflow_gap,\n        \"bellman_ledger_component_gaps\": dict(zip(COST_NAMES, component_gaps)),\n        \"state_count\": len(states),\n        \"transition_count\": transition_count,\n        \"event_ledger\": \"profit_per_good\": ledger_profit,\n            \"propagation_rounds\": ledger[\"propagation_rounds\"],\n            \"unresolved_probability\": ledger[\"unresolved_probability\"],\n        },\n        \"salvage_credit_once\": bool(SALVAGE_CREDIT_ONCE),\n    }\n\n\nevaluate_policy = evaluate_strategy\npolicy_evaluator = evaluate_strategy\n\n\ndef _optimal(rows):\n    feasible_rows = [row for row for row in feasible_rows if abs(row[\"profit\"] - best_profit) <= CASHFLOW_ABS_TOL_YUAN]\n    representatives = sorted(winners, key=lambda row: tuple(row[\"strategy_tuple\"]))\n    return representatives[0], winners, best_profit\n\n\ndef _with_defect_offset(pars, offset):\n    updated = copy.deepcopy(pars)\n    for part in (updated[\"part1\"], updated[\"part2\"], updated[\"product\"]):\n        part[\"defect_rate\"] = validate_probability(part[\"defect_rate\"] + offset)\n    return updated\n\n\ndef _with_cost_multiplier(pars, multiplier):\n    updated = copy.deepcopy(pars)\n    for part in (updated[\"part1\"], updated[\"part2\"]):\n        part[\"purchase_price\"] = validate_money(part[\"purchase_price\"] * multiplier)\n        part[\"inspection_cost\"] = validate_money(part[\"inspection_cost\"] * multiplier)\n    updated[\"product\"][\"assembly_cost\"] = validate_money(updated[\"product\"][\"assembly_cost\"] * multiplier)\n int(len(Q2_DECISION_SPACE) // int(Q2_EFFECTIVE_STRATEGY_COUNT) + ONE)\n    if point_count < int(ONE):\n        raise RuntimeError(\"invalid scan resolution\")\n    return [float(low + (high - low) * index / (point_count - ONE)) for index in range(point_count)]\n\n\ndef _evaluate_all(pars):\n    rows = [evaluate_strategy(pars, strategy) for strategy in iter_q2_policies()]\n    representative, winners, best_profit = _optimal(rows)\n    return representative, winners, best_profit\n\n\ndef _sensitivity_analysis(cases):\n    offsets = _scan_grid(-SENSITIVITY_DEFECT_OFFSET_ABS, SENSITIVITY_DEFECT_OFFSET_ABS)\n    multipliers = _scan_grid(SENSITIVITY_COST_MULTIPLIER_LOW, SENSITIVITY_COST_MULTIPLIER_HIGH)\n    defect_sensitivity = []\n    cost_sensitivity = []\n    for case in cases:\n        pars = case[\"parameters\"]\n        defect_profits = []\n        defect_strategies = []\n        for offset in offsets:\n            representative, _, profit = _evaluate_all(_with_defect_offset(pars, offset))\n            defect_profits.append(profit)\n            defect_strategies.append(representative[\"strategy\"])\n        cost_profits = []\n        cost_strategies = []\n        for multiplier in multipliers:\n            representative, _, profit = _evaluate_all(_with_cost_multiplier(pars, multiplier))\n            cost_profits.append(profit)\n for index, part_id in enumerate(PART_IDS) if part_id in children]\n        child_good = np.prod(leaf_good[:, indices], axis=1)\n        _, item_feasible = _internal_arrays(\n            np.sum(leaf_cost[:, indices], axis=1),\n            child_good,\n            parameters[semi_id],\n            components[\"semi_z\"],\n            components[\"semi_d\"],\n        )\n        repeated = np.repeat(item_feasible, len(SEMI_IDS) * len(components[\"root_options\"]))\n        semi_feasible = repeated if semi_feasible is None else semi_feasible & repeated\n    feasible &= semi_feasible\n    profits = np.where(feasible, parameters[root_id][\"sale_price\"] - root_cost, -np.inf)\n    return profits, feasible\n\n\ndef _policy_tuple(policy_id):\n    components = _policy_components()\n    leaf_code = int(components[\"leaf_code\"][policy_id])\n    semi_code = int(components[\"semi_code\"][policy_id])\n    root_code = int(components[\"root_code\"][policy_id])\n    leaf = tuple((leaf_code >> bit) & 1 for bit in range(len(PART_IDS)))\n    semi_base = len(components[\"semi_options\"])\n    semi_values = []\n    for _ in SEMI_IDS:\n        semi_values.append(components[\"semi_options\"][semi_code % semi_base])\n        semi_code //= semi_base\n    root = components[\"root_options\"][root_code]\n    return leaf + tuple(semi_values) + root\n\n\ndef _policy_dict(policy_id):\n    components = _policy_components()\n    leaf_code = int(components[\"leaf_code\"][policy_id])\n    semi_code = int(components[\"semi_code\"][policy_id])\n    root_code = int(components[\"root_code\"][policy_id])\n    leaf_index = 0\n    parts = {}\n    for node_id in PART_IDS:\n        inspect = (leaf_code >> leaf_index) & 1\n        parts[str(node_id)] = {\"inspect\": inspect, \"disassemble\": 0}\n        leaf_index += 1\n    semi_values = {}\n    semi_base = len(components[\"semi_options\"])\n    for node_id in SEMI_IDS:\n        inspect, disassembly = components[\"semi_options\"][semi_code % semi_base]\n        semi_values[str(node_id)] = {\"inspect\": inspect, \"disassemble\": disassembly}\n        semi_code //= semi_base\n    inspect, disassembly = components[\"root_options\"][root_code]\n    return {\n        \"parts\": parts,\n        \"semi_products\": semi_values,\n        \"finished_product\": {\"inspect\": inspect, \"disassemble\": disassembly},\n    }\n\n\ndef _best_policy(profits, feasible):\n    candidates = np.flatnonzero(feasible & np.isfinite(profits))\n    if candidates.size == 0:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"no feasible Q3 policy\")\n    maximum = float(np.max(profits[candidates]))\n    winners = candidates[np.abs(profits[candidates] - maximum) <= CASHFLOW_ABS_TOL_YUAN]\n    representative = min((int(item) for item in winners), key=_policy_tuple)\n    return representative, maximum, [int(item) for item in winners]\n\n\ndef _decision_from_mapping(mapping, section, node_id, default_disassembly=0):\n    if not isinstance(mapping, dict):\n        return 0, int(default_disassembly)\n    container = mapping.get(section, mapping)\n    if not isinstance(container, dict):\n        return 0, int(default_disassembly)\n    value = container.get(node_id, container.get(str(node_id)))\n    if value is None:\n        return 0, int(default_disassembly)\n    if isinstance(value, dict):\n        inspect = _field(value, (\"inspect\", \"z\", \"detection\"), 0)\n        disassembly = _field(value, (\"disassemble\", \"d\"), default_disassembly)\n    else:\n        inspect, disassembly = value\n    return int(inspect), int(disassembly)\n\n\ndef _graph_policy(mapping, node_id, kind):\n    if kind == \"part\":\n        return _decision_from_mapping(mapping, \"parts\", node_id, 0)\n    if kind == \"semi\":\n        return _decision_from_mapping(mapping, \"semi_products\", node_id, 0)\n    return _decision_from_mapping(mapping, \"finished_product\", node_id, 0)\n\n\ndef _direct_lot(node, graph, parameters, policy):\n    children = _ordered_children(graph, node)\n    inspect, disassembly = _graph_policy(policy, node, parameters[node][\"kind\"])\n    if not children:\n        rate = parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n        if inspect:\n            available = 1.0 - rate\n            if available <= 0.0:\n                return np.inf, 0.0, False\n            cost = (\n                parameters[node][\"purchase_price\"] + parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"]\n            ) / available\n            return cost, 1.0, True\n        return parameters[node][\"purchase_price\"], 1.0 - rate, True\n    child_lots = [_direct_lot(child, graph, parameters, policy) for child in children]\n    if any(not item[2] for item in child_lots):\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    child_cost = sum(item[0] for item in child_lots)\n    child_good = np.prod([item[1] for item in child_lots])\n    available = 1.0 - parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n    q = available * child_good\n    trial = child_cost + parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"]\n    if inspect == 0:\n        return trial, q, q > 0.0\n    if q <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    if disassembly == 0:\n        return (trial + parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"]) / q, 1.0, True\n    if child_good < 1.0 - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL or available <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    return (\n        trial + (parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"] + parameters[node][\"disassembly_cost\"]) / available,\n        1.0,\n        True,\n    )\n\n\ndef _event_lot(node, graph, parameters, policy):\n    children = _ordered_children(graph, node)\n    inspect, disassembly = _graph_policy(policy, node, parameters[node][\"kind\"])\n    if not children:\n        rate = parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n        if inspect:\n            available = 1.0 - rate\n            if available <= 0.0:\n                return np.inf, 0.0, False\n            return (\n                (parameters[node][\"purchase_price\"] + parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"]) / available,\n                1.0,\n                True,\n            )\n        return parameters[node][\"purchase_price\"], 1.0 - rate, True\n    lots = [_event_lot(child, graph, parameters, policy) for child in children]\n    if any(not item[2] for item in lots):\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    child_cost = sum(item[0] for item in lots)\n    child_good = float(np.prod([item[1] for item in lots]))\n    available = 1.0 - parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n    q = available * child_good\n    trial = child_cost + parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"]\n    if inspect == 0:\n        return trial, q, q > 0.0\n    if disassembly == 0:\n        if q <= 0.0:\n            return np.inf, 0.0, False\n        return (trial + parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"]) / q, 1.0, True\n    if child_good < 1.0 - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL or available <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    return (\n        trial + (parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"] + parameters[node][\"disassembly_cost\"]) / available,\n        1.0,\n        True,\n    )\n\n\ndef renewal_bellman_solver(child_cost, child_good, root, inspect, disassembly):\n    available = 1.0 - root[\"defect_rate\"]\n    q = available * child_good\n    if q <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    bad = 1.0 - q\n    branch_cost = 0.0\n    if inspect:\n        branch_cost += root[\"inspection_cost\"]\n    else:\n        branch_cost += root[\"exchange_loss\"] * bad\n    if disassembly:\n        branch_cost += root[\"disassembly_cost\"] * bad\n    if disassembly == 0:\n        cost = (child_cost + root[\"assembly_cost\"] + branch_cost) / q\n        return float(cost), 1.0, True\n    if child_good < 1.0 - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    fresh_cost = child_cost + root[\"assembly_cost\"] + root[\"inspection_cost\"] * inspect\n    fresh_cost += bad * root[\"disassembly_cost\"]\n    if inspect == 0:\n        fresh_cost += bad * root[\"exchange_loss\"]\n    reuse_cost = root[\"assembly_cost\"] + root[\"inspection_cost\"] * inspect\n    reuse_cost += bad * root[\"disassembly_cost\"]\n    if inspect == 0:\n        reuse_cost += bad * root[\"exchange_loss\"]\n    matrix = np.asarray([[q, -bad], [0.0, -bad]], dtype=float)\n    rhs = np.asarray([fresh_cost, reuse_cost], dtype=float)\n    solution = np.linalg.solve(matrix, rhs)\n    success_matrix = np.asarray([[q, -bad], [0.0, -bad]], dtype=float)\n    success_rhs = np.asarray([q, 0.0], dtype=float)\n    success = np.linalg.solve(success_matrix, success_rhs)\n    if not np.allclose(success[0], 1.0, atol=PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL, rtol=0.0):\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False\n    return float(solution[0]), float(success[0]), True\n\n\ndef _ledger_row(node, event_type, evidence, branch, probability, cash, state, reason):\n    return {\n        \"event_type\": event_type,\n        \"node\": node,\n        \"inspection_evidence\": evidence,\n        \"strategy_branch\": branch,\n        \"probability\": float(probability),\n        \"cash\": float(cash),\n        \"current_state\": state,\n        \"termination_reason\": reason,\n    }\n\n\ndef _ledger_lot(node, graph, parameters, policy):\n    children = _ordered_children(graph, node)\n    inspect, disassembly = _graph_policy(policy, node, parameters[node][\"kind\"])\n    if not children:\n        rate = parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n        available = 1.0 - rate\n        if inspect and available <= 0.0:\n            return np.inf, 0.0, False, []\n        rows = []\n        purchase_occurrences = 1.0 if inspect == 0 else 1.0 / available\n        inspection_occurrences = 0.0 if inspect == 0 else 1.0 / available\n        rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"purchase\", \"none\", \"leaf\", purchase_occurrences, -parameters[node][\"purchase_price\"], \"available\", \"consumed_or_screened\"))\n        rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"inspection\", \"perfect\", f\"z={inspect}\", inspection_occurrences, -parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"], \"screened\", \"screened\"))\n        good = 1.0 if inspect else available\n        return sum(item[2] * item[3] for item in rows), good, True, rows\n    lots = [_ledger_lot(child, graph, parameters, policy) for child in children]\n    if any(not item[2] for item in lots):\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False, []\n    child_cost = sum(item[0] for item in lots)\n    child_good = float(np.prod([item[1] for item in lots]))\n    rows = [row for item in lots for row in item[3]]\n    available = 1.0 - parameters[node][\"defect_rate\"]\n    q = available * child_good\n    if inspect == 0:\n        rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"assembly\", \"inputs_mixed_or_conditional\", \"z=0\", 1.0, -parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"], \"assembled\", \"parent_ready\"))\n        return child_cost + parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"], q, q > 0.0, rows\n    if q <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False, []\n    if disassembly == 0:\n        scale = 1.0 / q\n        rows = [dict(row, probability=row[\"probability\"] * scale) for row in rows]\n        rows.extend([\n            _ledger_row(node, \"assembly\", \"conditional\", \"z=1,d=0\", scale, -parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"], \"assembled\", \"geometric_continue\"),\n            _ledger_row(node, \"inspection\", \"perfect\", \"z=1,d=0\", scale, -parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"], \"screened\", \"geometric_continue\"),\n        ])\n        return (child_cost + parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"] + parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"]) * scale, 1.0, True, rows\n    if child_good < 1.0 - PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL or available <= 0.0:\n        return np.inf, 0.0, False, []\n    rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"assembly\", \"all_children_good\", \"z=1,d=1\", 1.0, -parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"], \"assembled\", \"recycle_continue\"))\n    rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"inspection\", \"perfect\", \"z=1,d=1\", 1.0 / available, -parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"], \"screened\", \"recycle_continue\"))\n    rows.append(_ledger_row(node, \"disassembly\", \"defective\", \"z=1,d=1\", (1.0 - available) / available, -parameters[node][\"disassembly_cost\"], \"disassembled\", \"recycle_continue\"))\n    return child_cost + parameters[node][\"assembly_cost\"] + (parameters[node][\"inspection_cost\"] + parameters[node][\"disassembly_cost\"]) / available, 1.0, True, rows\n\n\ndef event_cashflow_ledger(graph, parameters, policy):\n    root = _validate_topology(graph)\n    cost, good, feasible, rows = _ledger_lot(root, graph, parameters, policy)\n    inspect, _ = _graph_policy(policy, root, parameters[root][\"kind\"])\n    if feasible:\n        if inspect == 1 and _graph_policy(policy, root, parameters[root][\"kind\"])[1] == 0:\n            pass\n        rows.append(_ledger_row(root, \"sale\", \"terminal_good\", \"final\", 1.0, parameters[root][\"sale_price\"], \"terminal\", \"qualified_delivery\"))\n    return rows, feasible\n\n\ndef evaluate_policy(policy, parameters=None, graph=None, return_ledger=False, return_details=False):\n    parameters = parameters or _canonical_parameters()\n    graph = graph or build_h1_graph()\n    if isinstance(policy, (int, np.integer)):\n        policy = _policy_dict(int(policy))\n    root = _validate_topology(graph)\n    event_cost, event_good, event_feasible = _event_lot(root, graph, parameters, policy)\n    direct_lots = [_direct_lot(child, graph, parameters, policy) for child in _ordered_children(graph, root)]\n    direct_feasible = all(item[2] for item in direct_lots)\n    bellman_cost, bellman_good, bellman_feasible = renewal_bellman_solver(\n        sum(item[0] for item in direct_lots),\n        float(np.prod([item[1] for item in direct_lots])) if direct_feasible else 0.0,\n        parameters[root],\n        *_graph_policy(policy, root, parameters[root][\"kind\"]),\n    )\n    feasible = bool(event_feasible and bellman_feasible and direct_feasible)\n    gap = abs(event_cost - bellman_cost) if feasible else 0.0\n    profit = parameters[root][\"sale_price\"] - event_cost if feasible else -np.inf\n    rows = []\n    if feasible and return_ledger:\n        rows, _ = event_cashflow_ledger(graph, parameters, policy)\n        ledger_profit = sum(item[\"probability\"] * item[\"cash\"] for item in rows)\n        gap = max(gap, abs(ledger_profit - profit))\n    result = {\n        \"feasible\": feasible,\n        \"success_probability\": bellman_good if feasible else 0.0,\n        \"profit_per_good\": float(profit) if feasible else None,\n        \"profit_per_attempt\": float(profit * bellman_good) if feasible else None,\n        \"cashflow_gap\": float(gap),\n        \"rows\": rows,\n    }\n    return result if return_details or return_ledger else result[\"profit_per_good\"]\n\n\ndef _audit_all_h1_policies():\n    profits, feasible = vector_h1_profits()\n    graph = build_h1_graph()\n    parameters = _canonical_parameters()\n    maximum_gap = 0.0\n    worst_id = None\n    finite_count = 0\n    for policy_id in range(len(profits)):\n        if not \"worst_policy_id\": worst_id,\n        \"audited_feasible_count\": finite_count,\n        \"audited_infeasible_count\": int(len(profits) - finite_count),\n    }\n\n\ndef _q2_value(raw, flat_names, nested_name, nested_names, default=_MISSING):\n    for name in flat_names:\n        if name in raw:\n            return raw[name]\n    nested = raw.get(nested_name)\n    if isinstance(nested, dict):\n        for name in nested_names:\n            if name in nested:\n                return nested[name]\n    if default is not _MISSING:\n        return default\n    raise KeyError(f\"Q2 parameter not found: {flat_names}\")\n\n\ndef _q2_case_parameters(raw):\n    p1 = _rate(_q2_value(raw, (\"p_part1\", \"part1_defect_rate\", \"p_1\"), \"part1\", (\"次品率\", \"defect_rate\", \"p\")))\n    p2 =    g2 = _money(_q2_value(raw, (\"inspection_part2\", \"g_2\"), \"part2\", (\"检测成本\", \"inspection_cost\", \"g\")))\n    assembly = _money(_q2_value(raw, (\"assembly_product\", \"a_product\", \"a\"), sale = _money(_q2_value(raw, (\"sale\", \"sale_price\", \"S\"), \"product\", (\"市场售价\", \"sale_price\")))\n    exchange = _money(_q2_value(raw, (\"exchange\", \"exchange_loss\", \"L\"), \"product\", (\"调换损失\", \"exchange_loss\")))\n    disassembly = _money(_q2_value(raw, (\"disassembly_product\", \"disassembly\", \"h\"), \"product\", (\"拆解费用\", \"disassembly_cost\")))\n    case_id = _field(raw, (\"id\", \"case_id\", \"case\"), len(Q2_CASES))\n    return case_id, {\n        \"p1\": {\"id\": \"p1\", \"kind\": \"part\", \"defect_rate\": p1, \"purchase_price\": b1, \"inspection_cost\": g1},\n        \"p2\": {\"id\": \"p2\", \"kind\": \"part\", \"defect_rate\": p2, \"purchase_price\": b2, \"inspection_cost\": g2},\n        \"f\": {\n            \"id\": \"f\",\n            \"kind\": \"root\",\n            \"defect_rate\": pr,\n            \"assembly_cost\": assembly,\n            \"inspection_cost\": inspection,\n            \"disassembly_cost\": disassembly,\n            \"sale_price\": sale,\n            \"exchange_loss\": exchange,\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef _q2_policy(tuple_value):\n    return {\n        \"parts\": {\n            \"p1\": {\"inspect\": tuple_value[0], \"disassemble\": 0},\n            \"p2\": {\"inspect\": tuple_value[1], \"disassemble\": 0},\n        },\n        \"finished_product\": {\"inspect\": tuple_value[2], \"disassemble\": tuple_value[3]},\n    }\n\n\ndef _degeneration_audit():\n    maximum_gap = 0.0\n    worst_case = None\n    worst_strategy = None\n    audited = 0\n    graph = networkx.DiGraph()\n    graph.add_edge(\"p1\", \"f\")\n    graph.add_edge(\"p2\", \"f\")\n    for raw in Q2_CASES:\n        case_id, parameters = _q2_case_parameters(raw)\n        for strategy in Q2_DECISION_SPACE:\n            policy = _q2_policy(strategy)\n            event = evaluate_policy(policy, parameters, graph, return_details=True)\n            if not event[\"feasible\"]:\n                continue\n            audited += 1\n            gap = event[\"cashflow_gap\"]\n            if gap > maximum_gap:\n                maximum_gap = gap\n                worst_case = case_id\n                worst_strategy = list(strategy)\n            if gap > CASHFLOW_ABS_TOL_YUAN:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Q2/Q3 degeneration gap {gap}\")\n    if audited == 0:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"degeneration audit examined no feasible policy\")\n    return {\n        \"maximum_gap\": maximum_gap,\n        \"worst_case_id\": worst_case,\n        \"worst_strategy\": worst_strategy,\n        \"audited_policy_count\": audited,\n        \"case_count\": len(Q2_CASES),\n    }\n\n\ndef _replace_rates(parameters, rate_vector):\n    parameters = copy.deepcopy(parameters)\n    ids = list(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS)\n    if isinstance(rate_vector, dict):\n        values = []\n        for node_id in ids:\n            if node_id in rate_vector:\n                values.append(rate_vector[node_id])\n            elif str(node_id) in rate_vector:\n                values.append(rate_vector[str(node_id)])\n            else:\n                raise KeyError(f\"missing rate for {node_id}\")\n    else:\n        values = list(rate_vector)\n    if len(values) != len(ids):\n        raise ValueError(f\"expected {len(ids)} Q3 rates, received {len(values)}\")\n    for node_id, value in zip(ids, values):\n        if node_id not in parameters:\n            raise KeyError(f\"unknown Q3 rate node {node_id}\")\n        parameters[node_id][\"defect_rate\"] = _rate(value)\n    return parameters\n\n\ndef optimal_policy(rates=None, parameters=None, return_details=True):\n    parameters = parameters or _canonical_parameters()\n    if rates is not None:\n        parameters = _replace_rates(parameters, rates)\n    profits, feasible = vector_h1_profits(parameters)\n    representative, profit, winners = _best_policy(profits, feasible)\n    details = {\n        \"strategy\": _policy_dict(representative),\n        \"profit\": float(profit),\n        \"policy_id\": representative,\n        \"winner_policy_ids\": winners,\n    }\n    return details if return_details else (details[\"strategy\"], details[\"profit\"])\n\n\ndef _defect_variant(parameters, target, offset):\n    parameters = copy.deepcopy(parameters)\n    for node_id in PART_IDS if target == \"part_rates\" else SEMI_IDS if target == \"semi_rates\" else (FINISHED_PRODUCT_ID,):\n        parameters[node_id][\"defect_rate\"] = _rate(parameters[node_id][\"defect_rate\"] + offset)\n    return parameters\n\n\ndef _cost_variant(parameters, target, multiplier):\n    parameters = copy.deepcopy(parameters)\n    for node_id, node in parameters.items():\n        if target == \"purchase\" and node[\"kind\"] == \"part\":\n            node[\"purchase_price\"] *= multiplier\n        if target == \"inspection\":\n            node[\"inspection_cost\"] *= multiplier\n        if target == \"assembly\" and node[\"kind\"] != \"part\":\n            node[\"assembly_cost\"] *= multiplier\n        if target == \"exchange\" and node[\"kind\"] == \"root\":\n            node[\"exchange_loss\"] *= multiplier\n        if target == \"disassembly\" and node[\"kind\"] != \"part\":\n            node[\"disassembly_cost\"] *= multiplier\n    return parameters\n\n\ndef _sensitivity_analysis(parameters):\n    offsets = (-SENSITIVITY_DEFECT_OFFSET_ABS, 0.0, SENSITIVITY_DEFECT_OFFSET_ABS)\n    defect_rows = []\n    for target in (\"part_rates\", \"semi_rates\", \"root_rate\"):\n        for offset in offsets:\n            variant = _defect_variant(parameters, target, offset)\n            best = optimal_policy(parameters=variant)\n            defect_rows.append({\n                \"target\": target,\n                \"defect_offset\": float(offset),\n                \"profit\": best[\"profit\"],\n                \"policy_id\": best[\"policy_id\"],\n            })\n    cost_rows = []\n    for target in (\"purchase\", \"inspection\", \"assembly\", \"exchange\", \"disassembly\"):\n        for multiplier in SENSITIVITY_COST_MULTIPLIERS:\n            variant = _cost_variant(parameters, target, multiplier)\n            best = optimal_policy(parameters=variant)\n            cost_rows.append({\n                \"target\": target,\n                \"cost_multiplier\": float(multiplier),\n                \"profit\": best[\"profit\"],\n                \"policy_id\": best[\"policy_id\"],\n            })\n    grid_rows = []\n    for offset, multiplier in itertools.product(offsets, SENSITIVITY_COST_MULTIPLIERS):\n        variant = ONE_VALUE if recovered[position] == ZERO else ZERO),\n                            (ONE_VALUE, ONE_VALUE if recovered[position] == ONE_VALUE else ZERO),\n                        )\n                    )\n            immediate = acquisition_cost.copy()\n            immediate[TWO_VALUE] += assembly\n            if inspect_root:\n                immediate[ONE_VALUE] += root_inspection\n            for first_status, second_status in product(status_values, repeat=TWO_VALUE):\n                conditional_good = (\n                    (ONE_VALUE - pr)\n                    if first_status == ONE_VALUE and second_status == ONE_VALUE\n                    else ZERO\n                )\n                bad_probability = ONE_VALUE - conditional_good\n                next_state = (\n                    recovered_index[(first_status, second_status)]\n                    if recycle_root\n                    else ZERO\n                )\n                transition[state_index, next_state] += bad_probability\n                branch_cost = immediate.copy()\n                if not inspect_root:\n                    branch_cost[COST_NAMES_Q4.index(\"exchange\")] += exchange * bad_probability\n                if recycle_root:\n                    branch_cost[COST_NAMES_Q4.index(\"disassembly\")] += disassembly * bad_probability\n                rhs_cost[state_index] += conditional_good * branch_cost + bad_probability * branch_cost\n                rhs_success[state_index] += conditional_good\n        solved = np.linalg.solve(transition, np.column_stack((rhs_success, rhs_cost)))\n        success = solved[:, ZERO]\n        costs = solved[:, ONE_VALUE:]\n        initial_success = float(success[ZERO])\n        initial_costs = costs[ZERO]\n        if initial_success <= ZERO:\n            return None\n        profit_try = sale * initial_success - float(np.sum(initial_costs))\n        profit_per_good = profit_try / initial_success\n        direct_profit = sale - float(np.sum(initial_costs)) / initial_success\n        breakdown = {\n            name: float(initial_costs[index] / initial_success)\n            for index, name in enumerate(COST_NAMES_Q4)\n        }\n        return {\n            \"profit_per_good\": float(profit_per_good),\n            \"expected_profit\": float(profit_per_good),\n            \"profit_try\": float(profit_try),\n            \"success_probability\": initial_success,\n            \"unit_cost_breakdown\": breakdown,\n            \"identity_max_diff\": float(abs(profit_per_good - direct_profit)),\n            \"cashflow_gap\": float(abs(profit_per_good - direct_profit)),\n            \"feasible\": True,\n        }\n\n\ndef _profit_from_evaluation(value):\n    if not isinstance(value, dict) or __init__(self, policies):\n        self.policies = policies\n        self.leaf = np.asarray([policy[\"leaf_decisions\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        self.semi = np.asarray([policy[\"semi_decisions\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        self.root = np.asarray([policy[\"root_decision\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        self.module = callable(self.function)\n            else \"problem3_policy_space_fallback\"\n        )\n\n    def _dynamic(self, rates, rate_mapping, indices):\n        if not callable(self.function):\n            return None\n        selected_leaf = self.leaf[indices]\n        selected_semi = self.semi[indices]\n        selected_root = self.root[indices]\n        attempts = (\n            (rates, selected_leaf, selected_semi, selected_root),\n            (rate_mapping, selected_leaf, selected_semi, selected_root),\n            (rates, [self.policies[index] for index in indices]),\n        )\n        for arguments in attempts:\n            try:\n                value = self.function(*arguments)\n                if isinstance(value, dict):\n                    for key in (\"profits\", \"profit_per_good\", \"expected_profit\", \"profit_vector\"):\n                        if key in value:\n                            value = value[key]\n                            break\n                array = np.asarray(value, dtype=float)\n                if array.ndim > ONE_VALUE and array.shape[ZERO] == len(indices):\n                    array = array[ZERO]\n                array = array.reshape(-1)\n                if len(array) == len(indices) and np.all(np.isfinite(array)):\n                    return array\n            except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, FloatingPointError):\n                continue\n        return None\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _fallback(rates, policies, constants):\n        leaf = np.asarray([policy[\"leaf_decisions\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        semi = np.asarray([policy[\"semi_decisions\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        root = np.asarray([policy[\"root_decision\"] for policy in policies], dtype=int)\n        leaf_cost = np.zeros((len(policies), len(PART_IDS)), dtype=float)\n        leaf_good = np.ones_like(leaf_cost, dtype=float)\n        for position in range(len(PART_IDS)):\n            screened = leaf[:, position] != ZERO\n            rate = constants[\"part_rates\"][position]\n            leaf_cost[:, position] = np.where(\n                screened,\n                constants[\"part_purchase\"][position] / (ONE_VALUE - rate),\n                constants[\"part_purchase\"][position],\n            )\n            leaf_cost[:, position] += np.where(\n                screened,\n                constants[\"part_inspection\"][position] / (ONE_VALUE - rate),\n                ZERO,\n            )\n            leaf_good[:, position] = np.where(screened, ONE_VALUE, ONE_VALUE - rate)\n        semi_cost = leaf_cost.sum(axis=ONE_VALUE)\n        semi_good = np.ones(len(policies), dtype=float)\n        for position in range(len(SEMI_IDS)):\n            inspect = semi[:, position] != ZERO\n            semi_cost += constants[\"semi_assembly\"][position]\n            semi_cost += np.where(\n                inspect, constants[\"semi_inspection\"][position], ZERO\n            )\n            semi_good *= np.where(inspect, ONE_VALUE, ONE_VALUE - constants[\"semi_rates\"][position])\n            semi_cost += np.where(\n                semi[:, position] == len(Q3_SEMI_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS) - ONE_VALUE,\n                constants[\"semi_disassembly\"][position] * (ONE_VALUE - semi_good) / semi_good,\n                ZERO,\n            )\n            semi_cost /= semi_good\n        root_good = semi_good.prod(axis=ONE_VALUE) * (ONE_VALUE - constants[\"root_rate\"])\n        root_cost = semi_cost.sum(axis=ONE_VALUE) + constants[\"root_assembly\"]\n        inspect_root = root != ZERO\n        root_cost += np.where(inspect_root, constants[\"root_inspection\"], ZERO)\n        recycle_root = root >= len(Q3_ROOT_EFFECTIVE_OPTIONS) - TWO_VALUE\n        failure_cost = np.where(recycle_root, constants[\"root_disassembly\"], ZERO)\n        failure_cost += np.where(\n            ~inspect_root,\n            constants[\"exchange_loss\"] * (ONE_VALUE - root_good),\n            ZERO,\n        )\n        if np.any(root_good <= ZERO):\n            raise RuntimeError(\"H1 fallback encountered a zero-success policy\")\n        return (constants[\"sale_price\"] * root_good - root_cost - failure_cost) / root_good\n\n    def evaluate(self, rates, indices=None):\n        rate_vector tuple(float(value) for value in upper)\n    initial_rates = upper\n    initial_profits = np.asarray(profit_function(initial_rates), dtype=float)\n    best_index = int(np.argmin(initial_profits))\n    best_value = float(initial_profits[best_index])\n    best_rates = initial_rates\n    queue = []\n    counter = ZERO\n    midpoint = tuple((lo + hi) / TWO_VALUE for lo, hi in zip(lower, upper))\n    heapq.heappush(queue, (-max(hi - lo for lo, hi in zip(lower, upper)), counter, midpoint, lower, upper))\n    while queue:\n        _, _, middle, lo, hi = heapq.heappop(queue)\n        if max(high - low for low, high in zip(lo, hi)) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n            continue\n        values = np.asarray(profit_function(middle), dtype=float)\n        index = int(np.argmin(values))\n        value = float(values[index])\n        if value < best_value:\n            best_value = value\n            best_rates = middle\n        dimension = int(np.argmax([high - low for low, high in zip(lo, hi)]))\n        left_lo, left_hi = list(lo), list(hi)\n        right_lo, right_hi = list(lo), list(hi)\n        left_hi[dimension] = middle[dimension]\n        right_lo[dimension] = middle[dimension]\n        counter += ONE_VALUE\n        heapq.heappush(\n            queue,\n            (\n                -max(high - low for low, high in zip(left_lo, left_hi)),\n                counter,\n                tuple((a + b) / TWO_VALUE for a, b in zip(left_lo, left_hi)),\n                tuple(left_lo),\n                tuple(left_hi),\n            ),\n        )\n        counter += ONE_VALUE\n        heapq.heappush(\n            queue,\n            (\n                -max(high - low for low, high in zip(right_lo, right_hi)),\n                counter,\n                tuple((a + b) / TWO_VALUE for a, b in zip(right_lo, right_hi)),\n                tuple(right_lo),\n                tuple(right_hi),\n            ),\n        )\n    return best_rates, best_value\n\n\ndef _worst_q2(parameters, strategies, lower_rates, upper_rates, evaluator, force_local=False):\n    def evaluate(rates):\n        updated = _replace_q2_rates(parameters, rates)\n        values = []\n        for strategy in strategies:\n            row = evaluator.evaluate(\n                updated,\n                strategy,\n                parameters.get(\"case_id\"),\n     ],\n    }\n\n\ndef _candidate_indices(point_profits, robust_profits, policies, pool_width):\n    total = len(policies)\n    pool_width = min(int(pool_width), total)\n    candidates = set(\n        np.argsort(point_profits, kind=\"stable\")[-pool_width:].tolist()\n    )\n    candidates.update(\n        np.argsort(robust_profits, kind=\"stable\")[-pool_width:].tolist()\n    )\n    position_by_key float(alpha))\n    if key not in design_cache:\n        minimum_n = _minimum_cp_sample_size(\n            scenario_rate,\n            alpha,\n            Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH,\n            INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP,\n        )\n        expected = ClopperPearsonBoundary(minimum_n, alpha).expected_halfwidth(\n            scenario_rate\n        )\n        next_n = _next_feasible_cp_sample_size(\n            scenario_rate,\n            alpha,\n            Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH,\n            minimum_n,\n            INTEGER_SEARCH_CAP,\n        )\n        next_expected = (\n            ClopperPearsonBoundary(next_n, alpha).expected_halfwidth(scenario_rate)\n            if next_n is not None\n            else None\n        )\n        design_cache[key] = (minimum_n, expected, next_n, next_expected)\n    minimum_n, expected, next_n, next_expected = design_cache[key]\n    xi = int(rng.binomial(minimum_n, scenario_rate))\n    interval = ClopperPearsonBoundary(minimum_n, alpha).interval(xi)\n    p_hat = xi / minimum_n\n    return {\n        \"node_id\": str(node_id),\n        \"scenario_rate\": float(scenario_rate),\n        \"n\": int(minimum_n),\n        \"n_min\": int(minimum_n),\n        \"x\": xi,\n        \"xi\": xi,\n        \"p_hat\": float(p_hat),\n        \"sampling_center\": float(p_hat),\n        \"center_source\": \"initial_synthetic_binomial_observation\",\n        \"interval\": interval,\n        \"cp_interval\": interval,\n        \"expected_cp_halfwidth\": float(expected),\n        \"next_feasible_n\": next_n,\n        \"next_feasible_expected_cp_halfwidth\": next_expected,\n        \"alpha_node\": float(alpha),\n        \"target_cp_halfwidth\": float(Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH),\n    }\n\n\ndef _same_strategy(left, right):\n    return _policy_key_q2(left) == _policy_key_q2(right)\n\n\ndef _same_q3_policy(left, right):\n    return _policy_key_q3(left) == _policy_key_q3(right)\n\n\ndef _validate_payload(payload):\n    failures = []\n    checks = {}\n\n    def check(name, condition, detail=None):\n        checks[name] = bool(condition)\n        if not condition:\n            failures.append({\"check\": name, \"detail\": detail})\n\n    check(\"synthetic_scenario_label\", payload.get(\"claim_labels\") == [Q4_SCENARIO_LABEL])\n    check(\"q4_exact_interval_switch\", payload.get(\"exact_interval\", True))\n    check(\"q3_conditional_topology\", payload.get(\"q3\", {}).get(\"topology_label\") == Q3_TOPOLOGY_CLAIM_LABEL)\n    check(\"q3_effective_policy_count\", payload.get(\"q3\", {}).get(\"effective_policy_count\") == Q3_H1_EFFECTIVE_POLICY_COUNT)\n    check(\"q2_policy_space_size\", payload.get(\"q2\", {}).get(\"policy_space_size\") == Q2_DECISION_SPACE_SIZE)\n\n    alpha_q2 = Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE / Q2_EFFECTIVE_STRATEGY_COUNT\n    alpha_q3 = Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE / len(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS)\n    check(\"q2_bonferroni_alpha\", abs(alpha_q2 - Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n    check(\"q3_bonferroni_alpha\", abs(alpha_q3 - Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q3) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL)\n    check(\n        \"q2_joint_coverage\",\n        abs(Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE - Q2_EFFECTIVE_STRATEGY_COUNT * alpha_q2) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL,\n    )\n    check(\n        \"q3_joint_coverage\",\n        abs(Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE - len(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS) * alpha_q3) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL,\n    )\n\n    endpoint_ok = True\n    interval_ok = True\n    sample_ok = True\n    width_ok = True\n    next_width_ok = True\n    center_ok = True\n    for node in payload.get(\"minimum_sample_results\", []):\n        n = int(node[\"n\"])\n        xi = int(node[\"x\"])\n        if not ZERO <= xi <= n:\n            sample_ok = False\n        if abs(float(node[\"p_hat\"]) - xi / n) > PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n            sample_ok = False\n        expected_interval = ClopperPearsonBoundary(n, node[\"alpha_node\"]).interval(xi)\n        if any(abs(float(a) - float(b)) > PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL for a, b in zip(expected_interval, node[\"interval\"])):\n            interval_ok = False\n        expected_width = ClopperPearsonBoundary(n, node[\"alpha_node\"]).expected_halfwidth(\n            node[\"scenario_rate\"]\n        )\n        if expected_width > Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH + PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n            width_ok = False\n        if node.get(\"next_feasible_n\") is not None:\n            next_width = ClopperPearsonBoundary(\n                int(node[\"next_feasible_n\"]), node[\"alpha_node\"]\n            ).expected_halfwidth(node[\"scenario_rate\"])\n            if next_width > Q4_TARGET_CP_HALFWIDTH + PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n                next_width_ok = False\n        if abs(float(node[\"sampling_center\"]) - float(node[\"p_hat\"])) > PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL:\n            center_ok = False\n        if xi == ZERO:\n            endpoint_ok &= abs(node[\"interval\"][ZERO]) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n        if xi == n:\n            endpoint_ok &= abs(node[\"interval\"][ONE_VALUE] - ONE_VALUE) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n\n    for alpha in (Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2, Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q3):\n        n = int(payload[\"minimum_sample_results\"][ZERO][\"n\"])\n        zero_interval = ClopperPearsonBoundary(n, alpha).interval(ZERO)\n        full_interval = ClopperPearsonBoundary(n, alpha).interval(n)\n        endpoint_ok &= abs(zero_interval[ZERO]) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n        endpoint_ok &= abs(full_interval[ONE_VALUE] - ONE_VALUE) <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n\n    check(\"integer_sample_constraints\", sample_ok)\n    check(\"cp_interval_recomputation\", interval_ok)\n    check(\"cp_endpoint_branches\", endpoint_ok)\n    check(\"minimum_cp_width\", width_ok)\n    check(\"next_sample_width\", next_width_ok)\n    check(\"sampling_centers_are_point_estimates\", center_ok)\n    check(\n        \"sampling_center_rule\",\n        payload.get(\"sampling_center_rule\") == Q4_SAMPLING_CENTER_RULE,\n    )\n    check(\n        \"sampling_audit\",\n        all(\n            abs(float(row[\"sampling_center\"]) - float(row[\"p_hat\"]))\n            <= PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n            and int(row[\"repeated_draw_count\"]) == Q4_MONTE_CARLO_REPETITIONS\n            for row in payload.get(\"sampling_audit_records\", [])\n        ),\n    )\n    check(\"fixed_monte_carlo_budget\", payload.get(\"monte_carlo_repetitions\") == Q4_MONTE_CARLO_REPETITIONS)\n    check(\"fixed_random_seed\", payload.get(\"random_seed\") == RANDOM_SEED)\n    check(\n        \"q3_topology_status_preserved\",\n        payload.get(\"q3\", {}).get(\"topology_status\") == Q3_H1_TOPOLOGY_STATUS,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"passed\": not failures,\n        \"checks\": checks,\n        \"failures\": failures,\n        \"constraint_count\": len(checks),\n    }\n\n\ndef run(*args, **kwargs):\n    del args, kwargs\n    q2_inputs = _ordered_q2_inputs()\n    q2_policies = [_strategy_tuple_q2(raw) for raw in iter_q2_policies()]\n    if len(q2_policies) != Q2_EFFECTIVE_STRATEGY_COUNT:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"Q2 exhaustive policy generator did not produce sixteen policies\")\n    h1_policies = _h1_policies()\n    q2_evaluator = _Q2Evaluator()\n    h1_evaluator = _H1VectorEvaluator(h1_policies)\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(RANDOM_SEED)\n    design_cache = {}\n    q2_rate_keys = (\"p_part1\", \"p_part2\", \"p_product\")\n\n    for _, parameters in q2_inputs:\n        for rate_key in q2_rate_keys:\n            _node_record(\n                rate_key,\n                parameters[rate_key],\n                Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2,\n                rng,\n                design_cache,\n            )\n    for index, node_id in enumerate(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS):\n        _node_record(\n            node_id,\n            Q4_SCENARIO_LABEL if False else _q3_constants()[\"part_rates\"][ZERO]\n            if index < len(PART_IDS)\n            else _q3_constants()[\"semi_rates\"][index - len(PART_IDS)]\n            if index < len(PART_IDS) + len(SEMI_IDS)\n            else _q3_constants()[\"root_rate\"],\n            Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q3,\n            rng,\n            design_cache,\n        )\n\n    q2_internal = []\n    for case_id, parameters in q2_inputs:\n        nodes = [\n            _node_record(\n                rate_key,\n                parameters[rate_key],\n                Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2,\n                rng,\n                design_cache,\n            )\n            for rate_key in q2_rate_keys\n        ]\n        point_rates = [node[\"p_hat\"] for node in nodes]\n        interval_pairs = [node[\"interval\"] for node in nodes]\n        joint = BonferroniJointRegion(\n            interval_pairs,\n            Q4_JOINT_CONFIDENCE,\n            Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q2,\n            Q2_EFFECTIVE_STRATEGY_COUNT,\n        )\n        point_parameters = _replace_q2_rates(parameters, point_rates)\n        point_rows = []\n        for strategy in q2_policies:\n            row = q2_evaluator.evaluate(\n                point_parameters, strategy, case_id, force_local=False\n            )\n            if row is None:\n                continue\n            point_rows.append(\n                {\n                    \"strategy_tuple\": tuple(strategy),\n                    \"strategy\": _q2_strategy_payload(strategy),\n                    \"profit_per_good\": float(row[\"profit_per_good\"]),\n                    \"cashflow_gap\": float(row.get(\"cashflow_gap\", ZERO)),\n                    \"unit_cost_breakdown\": row.get(\"unit_cost_breakdown\", {}),\n                }\n            )\n        point_best = _best_row(point_rows)\n        worst_rates, worst_values = _worst_q2(\n            parameters,\n            [row[\"strategy_tuple\"] for row in point_rows],\n            joint.lower_rates(),\n            joint.upper_rates(),\n            q2_evaluator,\n            force_local=False,\n        )\n        robust_rows = [\n            {\n                \"strategy_tuple\": row[\"strategy_tuple\"],\n                \"strategy\": row[\"strategy\"],\n                \"profit_per_good\": float(worst_values[index]),\n                \"cashflow_gap\": row[\"cashflow_gap\"],\n                \"unit_cost_breakdown\": row.get(\"unit_cost_breakdown\", {}),\n            }\n            for index, row in enumerate(point_rows)\n        ]\n        robust_best = _best_row(robust_rows)\n        q2_internal.append(\n            {\n                \"case_id\": case_id,\n                \"parameters\": parameters,\n                \"nodes\": nodes,\n                \"point_rates\": point_rates,\n                \"worst_rates\": list(worst_rates),\n                \"point_strategy\": point_best[\"strategy_tuple\"],\n                \"point_profit\": \"joint_coverage_lower_bound\": joint.coverage_lower_bound,\n                \"counts_series\": [[node[\"x\"]] for node in nodes],\n                \"flip_counts\": [],\n                \"robust_change_counts\": [],\n                \"convergence\": [],\n                \"monotone_upper_worst\": all(\n                    value <= point_rows[index][\"profit_per_good\"] + PROBABILITY_ABS_TOL\n                    for index, value in enumerate(worst_values)\n                )\n                and np.allclose(\n                    np.asarray(worst_rates), list(q3_constants[\"part_rates\"])\n        + list(q3_constants[\"semi_rates\"])\n        + [q3_constants[\"root_rate\"]]\n    )\n    q3_nodes = [\n        _node_record(\n            node_id,\n            q3_scenario_rates[index],\n            Q4_ALPHA_NODE_Q3,\n            rng,\n            design_cache,\n        )\n        for index, node_id in enumerate(Q3_RATE_NODE_IDS)\n    ]\n    q3_joint =
